## 🌟 Why This Repository?

Most machine learning resources offer either abstract theory or fragmented code snippets. **1,000 AI Projects** provides a structured, production-minded bridge from concept to execution:

* 📦 **1,000 Self-Contained Project Folders**: Every project is an isolated module containing its own executable code, documentation, and dependencies.
* 🐍 **Executable Python Scripts (`main.py`)**: Clean, dependency-resolved code ready to run directly in terminal.
* 📓 **Interactive Jupyter Notebooks (`notebook.ipynb`)**: Pre-built with problem framing, execution blocks, and visual plots for Google Colab or VS Code.
* 🏗️ **Architectural Blueprints (`README.md`)**: Each project specifies Problem Framing, Baseline Models, Evaluation Metrics, Dataset Ideas, and 4 Production Upgrade Paths.
* 🌐 **Interactive Streamlit Web Apps (`app.py`)**: Live visual parameter tuning, metrics monitoring, and interactive regression plots.
* 🗂️ **Zero Bloat & 100% Offline-Friendly**: Clean, modular structure without unnecessary heavy dependencies.

## 🚀 Quickstart Guide

### 1. Clone the Repository
```bash
git clone https://github.com/HarshChoudhary2003/1000-AI-projects.git
cd 1000-AI-projects
```

### 2. Environment Setup
```bash
# Create and activate a Python virtual environment
python -m venv venv

# Windows:
.\venv\Scripts\activate
# macOS / Linux:
source venv/bin/activate
```

### 3. Run Any Project

Navigate into any project directory to run its script, launch its notebook, or start its Streamlit UI:

```bash
# Example 1: Run Linear Regression House Price Estimator
cd "01_Machine_Learning_Basic_Projects/PROJECT_0001_linear_regression_model_for_predicting_house_prices"
pip install -r requirements.txt
python main.py

# Example 2: Launch its interactive Streamlit Web Dashboard
streamlit run app.py

# Example 3: Run Monte Carlo Tree Search (Reinforcement Learning)
cd "../../07_Reinforcement_Learning_Projects/PROJECT_0252_monte_carlo_tree_search"
python main.py
```

## 🧭 Structured Learning Tracks

Not sure where to begin? Follow these recommended mastery pathways:

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 🔰 TRACK 1: Foundational & Applied Machine Learning (Projects 0001 – 0080)   │
│    • Linear/Logistic Regression → Decision Trees → Ensembles → XGBoost     │
└──────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘
                                       │
┌──────────────────────────────────────▼──────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 🧠 TRACK 2: Deep Learning, Computer Vision & NLP (Projects 0081 – 0240)      │
│    • PyTorch Basics → CNNs → YOLO → RNNs/LSTMs → Transformers & BERT       │
└──────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘
                                       │
┌──────────────────────────────────────▼──────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 🤖 TRACK 3: Reinforcement Learning & Autonomous Agents (Projects 0241–0280)  │
│    • Q-Learning → MCTS → DQN → Policy Gradients → Actor-Critic → PPO/DDPG   │
└──────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘
                                       │
┌──────────────────────────────────────▼──────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 🏢 TRACK 4: Industry AI, Generative Models & MLOps (Projects 0281 – 1000)   │
│    • Time Series → VAEs/Diffusion → GNNs → Healthcare/Finance → LLM Agents │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 🌐 Interactive Web Dashboards

Selected projects include interactive **Streamlit web applications** featuring live parameter tuning and real-time visualization:

* 🏡 **[Project 0001: House Price Valuation Dashboard](01_Machine_Learning_Basic_Projects/PROJECT_0001_linear_regression_model_for_predicting_house_prices/app.py)**  
  * Real-time property parameter sliders (Square Footage, Presets)
  * Live regression line + confidence envelope ($\pm 1\text{ RMSE}$)
  * Dynamic price-per-square-foot valuation metrics
  * *Run with:* `streamlit run 01_Machine_Learning_Basic_Projects/PROJECT_0001_linear_regression_model_for_predicting_house_prices/app.py`

* ⚖️ **[Project 0002: Logistic Regression Decision Dashboard](01_Machine_Learning_Basic_Projects/PROJECT_0002_logistic_regression_for_binary_classification/app.py)**  
  * Real-time classification threshold tuning ($\tau \in [0.05, 0.95]$) with dynamic Confusion Matrix
  * Live ROC curve with highlighted operating threshold point
  * Feature log-odds & odds ratios ($e^\beta$) horizontal weight chart
  * Single-instance live probability scorer with visual risk badge
  * *Run with:* `streamlit run 01_Machine_Learning_Basic_Projects/PROJECT_0002_logistic_regression_for_binary_classification/app.py`

* 🛍️ **[Project 0003: Customer Segmentation & K-Means Studio](01_Machine_Learning_Basic_Projects/PROJECT_0003_k_means_clustering_for_customer_segmentation/app.py)**  
  * Dynamic cluster count tuning ($K \in [2, 10]$) with automated Elbow & Silhouette analysis
  * Interactive 2D & 3D PCA cluster scatter projections with centroid inspection
  * Segment behavioral profiling with average spend, frequency, and recency summaries
  * *Run with:* `streamlit run 01_Machine_Learning_Basic_Projects/PROJECT_0003_k_means_clustering_for_customer_segmentation/app.py`

* 🌸 **[Project 0004: Iris Flower Classification Studio](01_Machine_Learning_Basic_Projects/PROJECT_0004_decision_tree_classifier_for_iris_dataset/app.py)**  
  * Complete decision tree architecture diagram & exportable text-based rule hierarchy
  * Interactive 2D decision boundary contours over any two morphological dimensions
  * Multi-class confusion matrix, Gini feature importances, and live botanical specimen classifier
  * *Run with:* `streamlit run 01_Machine_Learning_Basic_Projects/PROJECT_0004_decision_tree_classifier_for_iris_dataset/app.py`

* 🧠 **[Project 0005: Neural Network from Scratch Studio](01_Machine_Learning_Basic_Projects/PROJECT_0005_simple_neural_network_from_scratch/app.py)**  
  * Pure NumPy multi-layer perceptron engine with customizable activations (Sigmoid, Tanh, ReLU)
  * Dynamic synaptic connectivity diagram with positive/negative weight thickness encoding
  * Real-time loss & accuracy convergence trajectories across logic gates and continuous 2D manifolds
  * 2D decision boundary surface and hidden layer representation space (manifold disentanglement)
  * Single-sample forward & analytical backpropagation trace with live inference coordinate probe
  * *Run with:* `streamlit run 01_Machine_Learning_Basic_Projects/PROJECT_0005_simple_neural_network_from_scratch/app.py`

## 🤝 Contributing

Contributions, enhancements, and community improvements are welcome! If you would like to:
* Add a Streamlit or Gradio interactive web app to any project
* Optimize algorithmic convergence or integrate fresh benchmark datasets
* Improve explanations or visual plots in `notebook.ipynb`

Feel free to fork the repository, create a feature branch (`git checkout -b feature/amazing-feature`), and submit a Pull Request!
